function dashboard(){
 if(mode===&#x27;tf&#x27;)return `&lt;div class=&quot;section-heading&quot;&gt;&lt;h2&gt;${U(&#x27;項目を選んで学習&#x27;)}&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;button data-action=&quot;alltf&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;全問に挑戦&#x27;)} →&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;grid&quot;&gt;${DATA.categories.map((title,i)=&gt;{const qs=DATA.questions.filter(q=&gt;q.category===i),c=count(qs,&#x27;correct&#x27;);return `&lt;button class=&quot;tile&quot; data-cat=&quot;${i}&quot; style=&quot;--tone:${tones[i]}&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;tile-head&quot;&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;number&quot;&gt;${i+1}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;pill&quot;&gt;${qs.length} ${U(&#x27;問&#x27;)}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;tile-name&quot;&gt;${title}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;tile-foot&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;正解数&#x27;)} ${c} / ${qs.length}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;track&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;fill&quot; style=&quot;width:${c/qs.length*100}%&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/button&gt;`;}).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/div&gt;`;
 if(mode===&#x27;past&#x27;)return `&lt;div class=&quot;section-heading&quot;&gt;&lt;h2&gt;${U(&#x27;本試験の過去問&#x27;)}&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;button data-action=&quot;allpast&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;全問に挑戦&#x27;)} →&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p class=&quot;muted&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;正しい選択肢を1つ選んでください。&#x27;)}&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;grid&quot; style=&quot;margin-top:15px&quot;&gt;${DATA.past.map(q=&gt;`&lt;button class=&quot;tile&quot; data-past=&quot;${q.id}&quot; style=&quot;--tone:#357ea1&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;tile-head&quot;&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;number&quot;&gt;${q.num}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;pill&quot;&gt;${U(status(q.id)===&#x27;correct&#x27;?&#x27;正解&#x27;:status(q.id)===&#x27;wrong&#x27;?&#x27;不正解&#x27;:status(q.id)===&#x27;unanswered&#x27;?&#x27;未回答&#x27;:&#x27;未採点&#x27;)}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;tile-name&quot;&gt;${q.label}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;tile-foot&quot;&gt;${q.categories.map(i=&gt;DATA.categories[i]).join(&#x27; ／ &#x27;)}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/button&gt;`).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/div&gt;`;
 const wrong=ALL.filter(q=&gt;status(q.id)===&#x27;wrong&#x27;);const unanswered=ALL.filter(q=&gt;status(q.id)===&#x27;unanswered&#x27;);return `&lt;div class=&quot;section-heading&quot;&gt;&lt;h2&gt;${U(&#x27;間違えた問題&#x27;)} ${wrong.length}${U(&#x27;問&#x27;)}&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;${wrong.length?`&lt;p class=&quot;muted&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;解説を確認して、もう一度挑戦しましょう。&#x27;)}&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;actions&quot;&gt;&lt;button class=&quot;primary&quot; data-action=&quot;review&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;もう一度解く&#x27;)} →&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;grid&quot;&gt;${wrong.map(q=&gt;`&lt;button class=&quot;tile&quot; data-single=&quot;${q.id}&quot; style=&quot;--tone:#a75e61&quot;&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;tile-name&quot;&gt;${q.kind===&#x27;tf&#x27;?U(&#x27;○×問題&#x27;)+&#x27; &#x27;+q.num:q.label}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;tile-foot&quot;&gt;${q.kind===&#x27;tf&#x27;?DATA.categories[q.category]:U(&#x27;本試験の過去問&#x27;)}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;`).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/div&gt;`:`&lt;div class=&quot;empty&quot;&gt;&lt;h3&gt;${U(&#x27;まだ該当する問題はありません。&#x27;)}&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;p class=&quot;muted&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;問題を解いて採点すると、復習する問題が表示されます。&#x27;)}&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;`}${unanswered.length?`&lt;div class=&quot;actions&quot;&gt;&lt;button data-action=&quot;unanswered&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;未回答の問題&#x27;)} ${unanswered.length}${U(&#x27;問&#x27;)} →&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;`:&#x27;&#x27;}`;
}
function question(q,i){const graded=Boolean(results[q.id]),a=answers[q.id],s=results[q.id],label=s===&#x27;correct&#x27;?&#x27;正解&#x27;:s===&#x27;wrong&#x27;?&#x27;不正解&#x27;:&#x27;未回答&#x27;;return `&lt;article class=&quot;question&quot; id=&quot;q-${q.id}&quot; aria-label=&quot;問題${q.num}&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;q-top&quot;&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;q-num&quot;&gt;${q.kind===&#x27;tf&#x27;?U(&#x27;問題&#x27;)+&#x27; &#x27;+q.num:q.label}&lt;/span&gt;${graded?`&lt;span class=&quot;status-${s}&quot;&gt;${U(label)}&lt;/span&gt;`:`&lt;span class=&quot;muted&quot;&gt;${i+1} / ${active.ids.length}&lt;/span&gt;`}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;${q.kind===&#x27;tf&#x27;?&#x27;tf-layout&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;}&quot;&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;p class=&quot;q-text&quot;&gt;${q.text}&lt;/p&gt;${q.source?`&lt;p class=&quot;source&quot;&gt;${q.source}&lt;/p&gt;`:&#x27;&#x27;}&lt;/div&gt;${q.kind===&#x27;tf&#x27;?`&lt;div class=&quot;tf-options&quot; role=&quot;group&quot; aria-label=&quot;問題${q.num}の回答&quot;&gt;${[1,0].map(v=&gt;`&lt;button data-answer=&quot;${q.id}&quot; data-value=&quot;${v}&quot; aria-label=&quot;${v?&#x27;○ 正しい&#x27;:&#x27;× 誤り&#x27;}&quot; aria-pressed=&quot;${a===v}&quot; ${graded?&#x27;disabled&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;}&gt;${v?&#x27;○&#x27;:&#x27;×&#x27;}&lt;/button&gt;`).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/div&gt;`:&#x27;&#x27;}&lt;/div&gt;${q.case?`&lt;div class=&quot;case&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;${U(&#x27;事例&#x27;)}&lt;/strong&gt;${q.case}&lt;/div&gt;`:&#x27;&#x27;}${q.kind===&#x27;past&#x27;?`&lt;div class=&quot;options&quot; role=&quot;group&quot; aria-label=&quot;選択肢&quot;&gt;${q.options.map((text,j)=&gt;`&lt;button class=&quot;option&quot; data-answer=&quot;${q.id}&quot; data-value=&quot;${j+1}&quot; aria-pressed=&quot;${a===j+1}&quot; ${graded?&#x27;disabled&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;}&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;opt-num&quot;&gt;${j+1}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;${text}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;`).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/div&gt;`:&#x27;&#x27;}${graded?`&lt;div class=&quot;explanation&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;key&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;正答&#x27;)}：${q.kind===&#x27;tf&#x27;?(q.answer?&#x27;○&#x27;:&#x27;×&#x27;):q.answer} &lt;small class=&quot;muted&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;回答&#x27;)}：${a===undefined?U(&#x27;未回答&#x27;):q.kind===&#x27;tf&#x27;?(a?&#x27;○&#x27;:&#x27;×&#x27;):a}&lt;/small&gt;&lt;/div&gt;${q.kind===&#x27;tf&#x27;?`&lt;p&gt;${q.explanation}&lt;/p&gt;`:`&lt;h3&gt;${U(&#x27;選択肢ごとの解説&#x27;)}&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;ol&gt;${q.explanations.map((e,j)=&gt;`&lt;li&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;verdict ${q.answer===j+1?&#x27;status-correct&#x27;:&#x27;status-wrong&#x27;}&quot;&gt;${j+1}　${q.answer===j+1?&#x27;○&#x27;:&#x27;×&#x27;}&lt;/span&gt;${e}&lt;/li&gt;`).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/ol&gt;`}&lt;/div&gt;`:&#x27;&#x27;}&lt;/article&gt;`;}
function session(){const qs=active.ids.map(id=&gt;BYID[id]),done=qs.every(q=&gt;results[q.id]),n=qs.filter(q=&gt;answers[q.id]!==undefined).length,s=scoreQuestions(qs,answers);let related=active.category===null?[]:DATA.past.filter(q=&gt;q.categories.includes(active.category));return `&lt;button data-action=&quot;home&quot;&gt;← ${U(mode===&#x27;past&#x27;?&#x27;過去問一覧へ&#x27;:&#x27;項目一覧へ&#x27;)}&lt;/button&gt;&lt;section class=&quot;session-head&quot;&gt;&lt;h2 tabindex=&quot;-1&quot;&gt;${active.title}&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;p&gt;${U(qs.every(q=&gt;q.kind===&#x27;tf&#x27;)?&#x27;正しいと思う場合は○、誤りと思う場合は×を選んでください。&#x27;:&#x27;回答後に採点すると、正答と根拠を確認できます。&#x27;)}&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/section&gt;${done?`&lt;section class=&quot;result&quot; role=&quot;status&quot; tabindex=&quot;-1&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;${U(&#x27;得点&#x27;)}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;strong class=&quot;score&quot;&gt;${s.correct}&lt;/strong&gt; / ${s.total} ${U(&#x27;点&#x27;)} &lt;span class=&quot;muted&quot;&gt;（${Math.round(s.correct/s.total*100)}%）&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p&gt;${U(s.correct===s.total?&#x27;全問正解です。よく理解できています。&#x27;:&#x27;解説を確認して、もう一度挑戦しましょう。&#x27;)}&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;muted&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;不正解&#x27;)} ${s.wrong}　／　${U(&#x27;未回答&#x27;)} ${s.unanswered}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;actions&quot;&gt;&lt;button data-action=&quot;retry&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;もう一度解く&#x27;)}&lt;/button&gt;${s.wrong?`&lt;button data-action=&quot;sessionWrong&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;間違えた問題&#x27;)} ${s.wrong}${U(&#x27;問&#x27;)} →&lt;/button&gt;`:&#x27;&#x27;}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;`:&#x27;&#x27;}&lt;div class=&quot;session-tools&quot;&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;muted&quot; id=&quot;answered&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;回答済み&#x27;)} ${n} / ${qs.length}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;muted&quot;&gt;${done?U(&#x27;解答と解説&#x27;):U(&#x27;採点前です。回答を選んでください。&#x27;)}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;questions&quot;&gt;${qs.map(question).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/div&gt;${related.length?`&lt;section class=&quot;session-head&quot;&gt;&lt;h3&gt;${U(&#x27;この項目の関連過去問&#x27;)}&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;related&quot;&gt;${related.map(q=&gt;`&lt;button data-past=&quot;${q.id}&quot;&gt;${q.label} →&lt;/button&gt;`).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;`:&#x27;&#x27;}&lt;div class=&quot;bottom-bar&quot;&gt;&lt;span id=&quot;bottomCount&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;回答済み&#x27;)} ${n} / ${qs.length}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;tools&quot;&gt;&lt;button data-action=&quot;home&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;戻る&#x27;)}&lt;/button&gt;&lt;button class=&quot;primary&quot; data-action=&quot;${done?&#x27;retry&#x27;:&#x27;grade&#x27;}&quot;&gt;${U(done?&#x27;もう一度解く&#x27;:&#x27;採点する&#x27;)}&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;`;}
function render(){document.getElementById(&#x27;app&#x27;).innerHTML=(active?&#x27;&#x27;:header())+nav()+(active?session():dashboard())+(readError?`&lt;div class=&quot;notice&quot;&gt;${U(&#x27;保存した記録を読み込めませんでした。新しく開始します。&#x27;)}&lt;/div&gt;`:&#x27;&#x27;);footer();applySettings();}
document.addEventListener(&#x27;click&#x27;,e=&gt;{const b=e.target.closest(&#x27;button&#x27;);if(!b||b.disabled)return;
 if(b.dataset.mode){mode=b.dataset.mode;home();return;}
 if(b.dataset.cat!==undefined){const i=Number(b.dataset.cat);start(DATA.questions.filter(q=&gt;q.category===i),`${i+1}　${DATA.categories[i]}`,i);return;}
 if(b.dataset.past){mode=&#x27;past&#x27;;const q=BYID[b.dataset.past];start([q],q.label);return;}
 if(b.dataset.single){const q=BYID[b.dataset.single];start([q],q.kind===&#x27;tf&#x27;?U(&#x27;○×問題&#x27;)+&#x27; &#x27;+q.num:q.label);retry();return;}
 if(b.dataset.answer){const id=b.dataset.answer;answers[id]=Number(b.dataset.value);save();document.querySelectorAll(`[data-answer=&quot;${id}&quot;]`).forEach(x=&gt;x.setAttribute(&#x27;aria-pressed&#x27;,String(Number(x.dataset.value)===answers[id])));let n=active.ids.filter(id=&gt;answers[id]!==undefined).length;for(const el of [&#x27;answered&#x27;,&#x27;bottomCount&#x27;])document.getElementById(el).innerHTML=U(&#x27;回答済み&#x27;)+&#x27; &#x27;+n+&#x27; / &#x27;+active.ids.length;footer();return;}
 switch(b.dataset.action){case&#x27;home&#x27;:home();break;case&#x27;alltf&#x27;:start(DATA.questions,U(&#x27;○×問題&#x27;)+&#x27; 74&#x27;+U(&#x27;問&#x27;));break;case&#x27;allpast&#x27;:start(DATA.past,U(&#x27;本試験の過去問&#x27;)+&#x27; 9&#x27;+U(&#x27;問&#x27;));break;case&#x27;grade&#x27;:gradeQuestions(active.ids.map(id=&gt;BYID[id]));render();document.querySelector(&#x27;.result&#x27;)?.focus();document.querySelector(&#x27;.result&#x27;)?.scrollIntoView({behavior:&#x27;instant&#x27;,block:&#x27;start&#x27;});break;case&#x27;retry&#x27;:retry();break;case&#x27;review&#x27;:{const qs=ALL.filter(q=&gt;status(q.id)===&#x27;wrong&#x27;);start(qs,U(&#x27;間違えた問題&#x27;));retry();break;}case&#x27;sessionWrong&#x27;:{mode=&#x27;wrong&#x27;;const qs=active.ids.map(id=&gt;BYID[id]).filter(q=&gt;status(q.id)===&#x27;wrong&#x27;);start(qs,U(&#x27;間違えた問題&#x27;));retry();break;}case&#x27;unanswered&#x27;:{const qs=ALL.filter(q=&gt;status(q.id)===&#x27;unanswered&#x27;);start(qs,U(&#x27;未回答の問題&#x27;));retry();break;}case&#x27;reset&#x27;:if(confirm(&#x27;このブラウザに保存した回答と採点結果をすべて消去します。よろしいですか？&#x27;)){answers={};results={};history={};save();home();}break;}
});
save();render();
&lt;/script&gt;&lt;/body&gt;&lt;/html&gt;
"></iframe>
